Task 1 — Brute Force
Can you think of the simplest possible approach, even if it uses O(n) space? What data structure would let you compare values in reverse order?
Write your pseudocode for the brute force first. ^VJPA8TrT

1 → 2 → 3 → 2 → 1 ^l8p5WPf8

var isPalindrome = function(head) {
    const stack = []
    let current = head
    while ( current !== null ) {
        stack.push(current)
        current = current.next
    }
    
    current = head
    while ( current !== null ) {
        if ( stack[stack.length - 1] === current ) {
            stack.pop()
        }else {
            return false
        }
        current = current.next
    }
    
    return true
}; ^YmbN3nzJ

- While using stack the middle value is the problem in 
this case, we can push values but we need to know
when we reach middle value
- Becuase after middle value we have to check 
current.val and stack.top() and if they are same pop
otherwise dont offcourse when loop ends
- Stack length if 0 means its a palindrom otherwise not
- to find middle value i can use slow and fast pointer 


- Or we can simply push everything to stack and in second 
loop compare each pop with current node  ^Z32Lzrvn

Appraoch 1  ^nYXsvxIB

Why === failed: In JS, === on objects checks reference equality (same spot in memory), not value equality. Two different node objects with .val = 1 are still two different objects, so === returns false. ^qtuflamI

var isPalindrome = function(head) {
    const stack = []
    let current = head
    while ( current !== null ) {
        stack.push(current)
        current = current.next
    }
    
    current = head
    while ( current !== null ) {
        if ( stack[stack.length - 1].val === current.val ) {
            stack.pop()
        }else {
            return false
        }
        current = current.next
    }
    
    return true
}; ^hz3MNQ4g

Time complexity
- we have two loops so O(N) + O(N) = O(N)
Space Complexity
- Since using a stack so O(N) ^hS3B86B9

Appraoch 3 ^F8DYNwkB

- We can find middle node 
- then we can reverse linked list 
- finally compare the values of both linked lists and if same 
they are palindrom ^Xl6qVaml

middle ^OlUU1wfU

var getMiddle = function(head){
    let slow = head
    let fast = head
    while ( fast !== null && fast.next !== null ) {
        slow = slow.next
        fast = fast.next.next
    }
    return slow    
} ^YR8Qpr4Z

Step 2 — pseudocode for reversing a linked list from a given node. ^6jJfu2TQ

var reverseList = function(head) {
    let current = head
    let prev = null 
    while (current !== null) {
        let next_ = current.next
        current.next = prev
        prev = current
        current = next_
    }
    return prev
} ^2dop80Ob

Step 3 — combining all three into the final solution pseudocode. ^dHDtHSrE

var isPalindrome = function(head) {
    const middleNode = getMiddle(head)
    let tail = reverseList(middleNode)
    
    let current = head
    while ( current !== null && tail !== null ) {
        if (current.val !== tail.val ) {
            return false
        }
        current = current.next
        tail = tail.next
    }
    return true
};

var getMiddle = function(head){
    let slow = head
    let fast = head
    while ( fast !== null && fast.next !== null ) {
        slow = slow.next
        fast = fast.next.next
    }
    return slow    
}

var reverseList = function(head) {
    let current = head
    let prev = null 
    while (current !== null) {
        let next_ = current.next
        current.next = prev
        prev = current
        current = next_
    }
    return prev
} ^RGrirfIU
